# ============================================================================
# 本文件由 docs/gen_arf_warship_design.py 生成，手改会被覆盖。
# ============================================================================
# 高速战舰设计台 · 选装与汇总
# 变量说明：
#   arf_ws_cur            当前页签 1..4（炮舰/突击舰/无畏舰/辅助舰）
#   arf_ws_<cv>_<slot>    该槽位当前选项序号，0 = 未安装
#   arf_ws_<cv>_<slot>_<stat>  该槽位当前选项的属性贡献（供汇总求和）
#   arf_ws_<cv>_<slot>_fac     该槽位当前选项的工厂占用贡献
#   arf_ws_<cv>_cost      该舰种当前设计的总工厂占用（决议 available 直接读它）
#   arf_ws_<cv>_<stat>    该舰种的属性加成合计（面板摘要显示）
#   arf_ws_<cv>_<slot>_bn<atk|def|spd|org>  该槽位当前配件给的营级加成贡献
#   arf_ws_<cv>_<slot>_bnhp                  该槽位当前配件给的全军 HP 贡献
#   arf_ws_<cv>_bn<atk|def|spd|org> / _bnhp 该舰种的营级加成合计（面板摘要显示）
#   arf_ws_tmp / arf_ws_slot  循环与派发用的临时量

# ---------------- 打开 / 关闭设计台 ----------------
ARF_WS_Design_Open = {
	set_country_flag = arf_ws_design_open
	set_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 1 }
	ARF_WS_gb_recalc = yes
	ARF_WS_as_recalc = yes
	ARF_WS_dr_recalc = yes
	ARF_WS_ax_recalc = yes
}
ARF_WS_Design_Close = {
	clr_country_flag = arf_ws_design_open
}

# ---------------- 页签 ----------------

ARF_WS_gb_gun_calc = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_gb_gun = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_gb_gun_soft = 8 }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_gb_gun_hard = 6 }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_gb_gun_ap = 5 }

ARF_WS_gb_gun_prev = {
	set_variable = { arf_ws_tmp = arf_ws_gb_gun }
	subtract_from_variable = { arf_ws_tmp = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_tmp < 0 } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_tmp = 5 }
	}
	ARF_WS_gb_gun_apply = yes
}
ARF_WS_gb_gun_next = {
	set_variable = { arf_ws_tmp = arf_ws_gb_gun }
	add_to_variable = { arf_ws_tmp = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_tmp > 5 } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_tmp = 0 }
	}
	ARF_WS_gb_gun_apply = yes
}
ARF_WS_gb_gun_apply = {

	}
	ARF_WS_as_sec_apply = yes
}

	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 3 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_dr_ammo_next = yes
	}
	else = {
		ARF_WS_ax_ammo_next = yes
	}
}
ARF_WS_Slot_sp_prev = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 1 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_gb_sp_prev = yes
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 2 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_as_sp_prev = yes
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 3 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_dr_sp_prev = yes
	}
	else = {
		ARF_WS_ax_sp_prev = yes
	}
}

	}
}

# ---------------- 清空某一舰种的全部配件 ----------------
ARF_WS_Reset_Current = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 1 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_gb_gun_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_gb_sec_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_gb_arm_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_gb_eng_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_gb_fc_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_gb_ammo_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_gb_sp_0 = yes
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 2 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_as_gun_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_as_sec_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_as_arm_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_as_eng_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_as_fc_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_as_ammo_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_as_sp_0 = yes
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 3 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_dr_gun_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_dr_sec_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_dr_arm_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_dr_eng_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_dr_fc_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_dr_ammo_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_dr_sp_0 = yes
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_cur = 4 compare = equals } }
		ARF_WS_ax_gun_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_ax_sec_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_ax_arm_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_ax_eng_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_ax_fc_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_ax_ammo_0 = yes
		ARF_WS_ax_sp_0 = yes
	}
}

# ---------------- 开工：当前设计 → 产能占用 ----------------
# 动态修正只吃扁平的 变量 → 修正值，所以这里把「占用 N 座军工厂」
# 折算成 industrial_capacity_factory = -N ÷ 全国军工厂数。
# 若 N 超过全国军工厂数（决议 available 已拦住，这里再兜一层），
# 上限取全国军工厂数，避免出现低于 -100% 的产能。
ARF_WS_Penalty_gb = {
	# 兜底：老存档 / 从未打开过设计台时变量为空，先补成裸装基础值
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_gb_cost < 1 } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_gb_cost = 2 }
	}
	# 占用座数 > 全国军工厂总数时钳到总数，避免算出低于 -100% 的产能
	set_variable = { arf_tmp_pen = arf_ws_gb_cost }
	subtract_from_variable = { arf_tmp_pen = num_of_military_factories }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_tmp_pen > 0 } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_gb_cost_eff = num_of_military_factories }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { arf_ws_gb_cost_eff = arf_ws_gb_cost }
	}
	set_variable = { arf_pen_gunboat = arf_ws_gb_cost_eff }
	divide_variable = { arf_pen_gunboat = num_of_military_factories }
	multiply_variable = { arf_pen_gunboat = -1 }
}
ARF_WS_Penalty_as = {
	# 兜底：老存档 / 从未打开过设计台时变量为空，先补成裸装基础值
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_ws_as_cost < 1 } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_as_cost = 5 }
	}
	# 占用座数 > 全国军工厂总数时钳到总数，避免算出低于 -100% 的产能
	set_variable = { arf_tmp_pen = arf_ws_as_cost }
	subtract_from_variable = { arf_tmp_pen = num_of_military_factories }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { arf_tmp_pen > 0 } }
		set_variable = { arf_ws_as_cost_eff = num_of_military_factories }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { arf_ws_as_cost_eff = arf_ws_as_cost }
	}
	set_variable = { arf_pen_assault = arf_ws_as_cost_eff }
	divide_variable = { arf_pen_assault = num_of_military_factories }
	multiply_variable = { arf_pen_assault = -1 }
}

	# 兜底：老存档 / 从未打开过设计台时变量为空，先补成裸装基础值
